# 图素材手册（figure-book）

> 用途：为「隐藏工作区内容上传管线」横向报告收集全部配图素材——流程图、时间线、举证墙、宣称对照墙所需的原始数据在此一册收齐。**本册只收集整理数据，不画图。** 每个目标的全部字段逐字摘自对应 `figure-pack.json`，不重述、不修饰；编辑性内容仅出现在 §0（约定）、§10（横向矩阵）与 §11（作图清单）。

## 0. 数据出处与引用约定

### 0.1 数据源文件

| 目标              | pack 文件                                                        |
| ----------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------- |
| ZCode（对照基线） | `~/src/agent-teardown/evidence/zcode/figure-pack.json`           |
| Copilot Chat      | `~/src/agent-teardown/evidence/copilot-chat/figure-pack.json`    |
| Cursor            | `~/src/agent-teardown/evidence/cursor/figure-pack.json`          |
| CodeBuddy IDE     | `~/src/agent-teardown/evidence/codebuddy-ide/figure-pack.json`   |
| CodeBuddy CLI     | `~/src/agent-teardown/evidence/codebuddy-cli/figure-pack.json`   |
| Kiro              | `~/src/agent-teardown/evidence/kiro/figure-pack.json`            |
| Qoder             | `~/src/agent-teardown/evidence/qoder/figure-pack.json`           |
| Trae intl         | `~/src/agent-teardown/evidence/trae-intl/figure-pack.json`       |
| MiniMax Desktop   | `~/src/agent-teardown/evidence/minimax-desktop/figure-pack.json` |

一致性参照：`notes/sweep/deep-report.md`（8 家深剖报告 + 横向矩阵）、`notes/sweep/timeline.md`（版本考古汇总与覆盖缺口）。审计窗口 2026-09；全部静态分析，引用 file:line 相对 `~/src/agent-teardown/extracted/<tid>/`（exhibits 的 `file` 字段为绝对路径，原样收录）。

### 0.2 锚点约定

- `file:line`：举证片段（§\*.3）中 `file` 字段为绝对路径 + 行号区间；`code_anchor` 列内的形如 `v2.3.0:app/out/host/index.js:13762` 表示「版本:解包相对路径:行号」；`refs/ZCode/...` 指向 zai-org/ZCode 开源源码树；minified bundle 内位置记为 `@<字节偏移>` 或 `~<近似行>`。
- `stage` 有序表六列：`stage`（阶段名）/ `detail`（该阶段行为）/ `endpoint`（出网端点，无则标"无"）/ `gate`（门控表达式）/ `wire`（线上字节描述）/ `code_anchor`（代码锚点）。
- `state` 取值：`absent`（特征缺席）/ `present`（管线存在但非默认开或无法判定默认）/ `default-on`（生效配置下默认开）/ `removed`（功能族移除）。
- `verdict` 取值：`honest`（如实）/ `understated`（轻描淡写）/ `misleading`（误导）/ `contradicted`（与代码直接矛盾）/ `absent`（无任何对应宣称）。

### 0.3 重构样本标注约定

- §\*.2 的「重构载荷样本」是**按代码逐字段重构的线上字节示意**，非抓包记录：字段名、端点、头名全部逐字来自反编译代码；`<…>` 为取值占位；`②/③` 等圈号标注时序；「`//`」「`#`」后注释为整理者所加，与样本体本身区分。
- §\*.3 举证片段的 `quote` 为**逐字摘录**（代码原样、笔记表格原样），不修饰不翻译；`why` 为该条的图注（caption）。
- 表格内长文本中的 `<br>` 为单元格内换行转义；`\|` 为字面竖线转义。

---

截 100B、mode 恒 0644、uid/gid 恒 0、uname/gname/devmajor/devminor 零字节、mtime=Date.now()（打包时刻非文件 mtime）、ustar\\0+00、checksum=字节和；ustar 拆分失败才发 pax（x 型 PaxHeaders/${index}+path= 记录无转义，含 \\n 名破坏帧）；>255B 名走 pax。gzip createGzip() 默认（MTIME=0/XFL=0/OS=3）。v3.2.0+ 流式写 waitForStreamDrain+逐条 size/mtime 复查（wrote!==size\|\|size'!==size\|\|mtimeMs'!==mtimeMs→`repo snapshot file changed while packing`；ctime | 5. 加密+落盘（encrypt+persist：AES-256-CTR + RSA-OAEP wrap → pending）     | encryptArchive：随机 dataKey，createCipheriv("aes-256-ctr",…)，产物=nonce(16B)‖AES-256-CTR(dataKey)(tar.gz)，无认证（无 setAAD 调用——aad 字段只是随 envelope 序列化的声明性元数据）；dataKey 经 publicEncrypt({key:publicKeySpkiPem,padding:RSA_PKCS1_OAEP_PADDING,oaepHash:"sha256"})→base64 encryptedDataKey；plaintextSha256=对明文 tar.gz 算的 sha256（服务端解密前完整性锚定+去重，全链唯一完整性机制）。SPKI 解析失败换 RSA PUBLIC KEY(PKCS1) 重试。envelope=repo_snapshot_encrypted_artifact/v1\|v2{contentAlgorithm,keyWrapAlgorithm,keyId:String(key_version),nonceEncoding:"ciphertext-prefix-16-byte",aadEncoding:"canonical-json-v1",aad:{schema,workspaceKeyHash,kind,manifestHash,baseManifestHash?,compression:"tar.gz"},encryptedDataKey,plaintextSha256}。落盘：pending 文件名=groupId（v3.2.0+ ${manifestHash}.${createdAt}，v3.11.1+ 中段插 extraManifestHash）；根目录 <config>/repo-snapshots/（v2.3.0–v3.1.3）→<config>/checkpoints/（v3.2.0 renameSync 迁移，与 GitCheckpointStore 共址同 credential HTTP）→buildObjectUploadTarget：v3.2.1+ encryptedSizeBytes>data.max_size→payload_too_large，缺 snapshot_id→invalid。返回 set("file",openAsBlob(artifactPath),"repo-snapshot.tar.gz.enc")（application/octet-stream）。fetch：globalThis.fetch（→v3.12.1）→undici fetch+redirect:"error"（v3.12.2+）；不走 proxy-aware transport（v3.12.3 实测 POST）。失败 reason 产出枚举：invalid/key_expired(v3.6.1+)/payload_too_large(v3.2.1+)/object_upload_failed；服务端真拒收时客户端无感知照常 markAcceptedManifest（base_not_found/base_invalid/hash_mismatch 三分支自 v2.3.0 死代码=单向记账盲点）。 圈定 key/expire——OSS 侧接受与否经 HTTP status 表达，业务拒收经 callback 表达但客户端不读 Authorization/x-request-id/指纹头（不走 apiClient）；客户端 IP/时序/blob 大小对 OSS 可见。入站：HTTP status+etag（body 不解析） | 7. 注册（register：OSS callback——唯一登记通道，无独立 client→server 调用） | encodeOssCallback：callback 表单字段=base64(JSON.stringify({callbackUrl:callback.url,callbackBody:<替换后>,callbackBodyType:callback.content*type}))；replaceOssCallbackPlaceholders 以 /\\$\\{([^}]+)\\}/g 替换、Object.hasOwn 守卫、值 encodeURIComponent、未知占位符原样透传（模板本体来自服务端，客户端只填值）。占位符全史：update_type（baseline→full/increment→incremental，toServerUpdateType）、checksum=sha256:<plaintextSha256 明文 tar.gz 哈希>、encrypted_aes_key=wrap 后 dataKey base64（全版本）；x: 镜像三件套（全版本）；x:base_snapshot_id（v2.13.0+）；sessionId/queryId/requestId+x:（v3.1.0+，sessionId=taskId 剥 sess*）；failureCount+x:（v3.2.1+，String 密文得全部明文；checksum 让其解密前做完整性锚定与内容去重；workspace_id+x: 归因串联同仓全部上传与会话轨迹。客户端永远不知道服务端是否接受（response 体不读）。                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     | callbackBody（encodeURIComponent 填充后）：update_type/checksum=sha256:<plaintextSha256>/encrypted_aes_key(wrapped dataKey)/x:\* 归因（sessionId,queryId,requestId,failureCount,captureStage,historyRoundCount,base_snapshot_id）——wrapped key+明文哈希+会话归因=服务端解包+记账所需的一切                                                                                                                                          | v2.3.0:app/out/host/index.js repoSnapshotUploadClient.ts ~50265（encodeOssCallback/replaceOssCallbackPlaceholders，v3.12.3 同名 minified）；refs/ZCode/packages/services/src/feedback/feedbackHttpClient.ts:1054-1070（buildOssFormFields 同构字段名旁证） #### 1.1.2 diagram_nodes 序列

1. client:user action（prompt send / task complete / wiki gen / steer / cron / off-peak）
2. client:sendSession host RPC → scheduleRepoSnapshotSidecar（v2.x: runPrompt→captureBeforePrompt fire-and-forget）
3. client:RepoSnapshotCaptureIntentScheduler（v3.6.1+；32 深/120s/5s，AbortSignal.any）
4. client:captureBeforePromptUnsafe（throwIfAborted→tokenProvider JWT→workspaceKeyHash=`sha256(identity‖path)[:12]`→getUploadKey）
5. ── wire: HTTPS GET zcode.z.ai/api/v1/snapshot/upload-credential?workspace_id=（Bearer JWT + v3.1.0+ X-\* 指纹头）──
6. server:zcode.z.ai credential issuer（STS mint + RSA 公钥 + snapshot_id + callback 模板 + max_size；data:null=kill switch）
7. ── wire: credential JSON {callback,oss,encryption,snapshot,max_size} ──
8. client:scanner（git ls-files --cached --others --exclude-standard -z → walkFiles 回落；v3.1.0+ appendRootGitMetadataPaths 根 .git/ 零剪枝）
9. client:filter chain（symlink/.git→include/dep-cache-build/secret/>1MiB/NUL-binary）+ manifest/delta（path+sizeBytes）
10. client:tar+gzip packer（根目录=snapshot*id；meta/*+files/\_+extra-\*；手写 ustar+pax，tmp→rename）
11. client:encryptArchive（nonce‖AES-256-CTR(tar.gz) + RSA-OAEP-SHA256 wrap dataKey + plaintextSha256）
12. client:pending store（<config>/{repo-snapshots|checkpoints}/<hash12>/pending/<groupId>.enc+.envelope.json+state.json）
13. client:flushWorkspace→requestUploadTarget（内存查表 credential；miss→key_expired）
14. ── wire: HTTPS POST multipart → oss.host（policy/x-oss-\*/key/security-token + bare 归因明文 + callback base64 + file 密文；无 Authorization/指纹头）──
15. oss:Aliyun OSS bucket（PostObject；可见密文 blob+明文归因表单字段+base64 callback）
16. ── wire: OSS 服务端 POST callbackBody → callback.url ──
17. server:zcode.z.ai register/receive（unwrap encrypted_aes_key→dataKey→解明文；checksum 锚定+去重；workspace_id+x:\* 归因串联；响应客户端从不解析）

#### 1.1.3 secondary_channels

- 反馈附件 OSS 管线：POST /api/v1/feedback/attachment/upload-credential → OSS PostObject（----zcode-feedback-<uuid> 边界、日志附件≤1GiB）——用户主动发起，v3.14.0 后仍存活，与快照管线同构勿混淆（refs/ZCode/packages/services/src/feedback/feedbackHttpClient.ts:512-544,944-954,1054-1079 + feedbackService.ts:43 baseUrl=/api/v1）
- ARMS 遥测：proj-xtrace-\*.cn-beijing.log.aliyuncs.com/apm/trace/opentelemetry（OTLP，service zcode-cli-agent）+ /rum/web/v2 经 arms:rum-bridge IPC——可看到 credential GET 的 host+path 与成败/时延（normalizeHttpInterface 剥 query，workspace_id 不进遥测）；路径段白名单残留 snapshot/upload-credential 死段（refs/ZCode networkTelemetryAggregator.ts:113,119）
- 产品事件上报：POST zcode.z.ai/api/v1/event/report——userActionTraceCatalog 目录化 UI 动作（含 settings.indexing 拨开关 ui_action span），带真实 user_id+Authorization+device_mid 持久指纹
- 更新与远程配置：GET /api/v1/releases/electron/manifest → CDN（cdn-zcode.z.ai/cdn.codegeex.cn/cdn.zcode-ai.com）；GET /api/v1/{agent,client}/configs（forceUpdateGuard minimalVersion 可封停低版本）+ /api/v1/client/scenes + /api/v1/mcp/usage
- 远程工作区/远控：GET /api/server-info + /api/rpc-host-capability → wss://zcode.z.ai/ws/host（v4 会话流协议，desktop-continuous/web-remote-replayable 双信任级，32B 一次性 ticket 提权）
- 模型流量与网关改写：per-provider base（api.z.ai/open.bigmodel.cn 等）；official-coding-plan-gateway 把指向 api.z.ai/open.bigmodel.cn 的 anthropic URL 透明重定向至 zcode.z.ai/api/v1/ultra(-zai)/anthropic/v1/messages 含完整 auth 头（refs/ZCode apps/zcode-cli/packages/adapters/src/model/official-coding-plan-gateway.ts:22-29）
- OAuth 代理：POST zcode.z.ai/api/v1/oauth/token（服务端 broker）+ chat.z.ai/api/oauth/{authorize,userinfo} + api.z.ai/api/auth/z/login + bigmodel.cn/api/auth/tokenByAuthCode；2.x 内嵌 BIGMODEL_LEGACY_APP_SECRET_FALLBACK 明文（extracted/2.13.0/app/out/host/index.js:44137，3.x 移除）
- 营销与计费：v3.14.0 新增 GET /api/v1/marketing/touch + POST /api/v1/marketing/touch/action；/api/v1/off-peak/{ticket*,anthropic/v1/messages}、/api/v1/zcode-plan/*、/api/v1/coding-plan/*、bigmodel.cn/api/biz/* 配额
- IM bot 远程投递：open.feishu.cn/accounts.feishu.cn OAuth + api.telegram.org——`bots:task*` IPC + `zc:*` callback_data 远程驱动任务
- 2.x 本地 MITM 面（非厂商 egress 但抓全量 agent-provider 流量）：acp-proxy-runtime 自签根 CA 'ZCode ACP HTTPS Proxy Root CA' 注入 bundled agent 信任库 + AcpProxyTrafficStore 抓包（3 天留存），3.1.0 整体移除；源码期确认同样存在 ensureAppCaCert 本地 MITM CA（自签根 CA，私钥供出口代理 TLS 重签）注入 NODE_EXTRA_CA_CERTS（refs/ZCode/packages/services/src/runtime-tools/appCaCert.ts:33 + packages/services/src/node.ts:1387）

### 1.2 重构载荷样本

**一句量级估算**：每次捕获离机密文 ~0.3–5MB（小仓）至 20–300MB（带成熟 .git 整树），空仓也发 meta-only ~1–2KB；按 prompt 触发频率线性放大，活跃日 50MB–10GB。

```text
重构样本 —— v3.12.3 一次 prompt 触发（captureStage:"prompt"）increment 上传的全部离机字节。字段名全部逐字来自代码/nodes 报告记录；<…> 为取值占位。注：callback.body 模板本体由服务端下发，结构示意仅标出已知 ${key} 占位键集。

== ① client → zcode.z.ai（GET，唯一鉴权一跳；v3.1.0+ 带指纹头） ==
GET /api/v1/snapshot/upload-credential?workspace_id=a1b2c3d4e5f6 HTTP/1.1
Host: zcode.z.ai
Authorization: Bearer <zcodeJwtToken ?? accessToken>
User-Agent: ZCode/3.12.3
HTTP-Referer: <origin>
X-Title: Z Code@electron
X-ZCode-App-Version: 3.12.3
X-Platform: linux
X-Release-Channel: stable
X-Client-Language: zh-CN
X-Client-Timezone: Asia/Shanghai
X-Os-Category: linux
X-Os-Version: <os ver>
X-Device-Mid: <uuid>
x-request-id: <uuidv4>

== ② server → client（credential 响应，{code,msg,data} 信封；resolveUploadCredentialData 必填校验） ==
{
  "code": 0, "msg": "ok",
  "data": {
    "callback": {
      "url": "https://zcode.z.ai/<登记路径>",
      "body": "<服务端模板，含 ${update_type} ${checksum} ${encrypted_aes_key} ${x:update_type} ${x:checksum} ${x:encrypted_aes_key} ${x:base_snapshot_id} ${sessionId} ${x:sessionId} ${queryId} ${x:queryId} ${requestId} ${x:requestId} ${failureCount} ${x:failureCount} ${captureStage} ${x:captureStage} ${historyRoundCount} ${x:historyRoundCount}>",
      "content_type": "application/json"
    },
    "oss": {
      "host": "https://<bucket>.oss-cn-<region>.aliyuncs.com",
      "path": "<object key>",
      "policy": "<base64 policy>",
      "x_oss_signature": "<OSS4-HMAC-SHA256 sig>",
      "x_oss_signature_version": "<ver>",
      "x_oss_credential": "<ak>/<date>/<region>/oss/aliyun_v4_request",
      "x_oss_security_token": "<STS token>",
      "x_oss_date": "<UTC>"
    },
    "encryption": {
      "public_key": "-----BEGIN PUBLIC KEY-----\n<SPKI b64>\n-----END PUBLIC KEY-----",
      "key_version": 3,
      "algorithm": "RSA-OAEP-256"
    },
    "snapshot": { "snapshot_id": "<sid>", "base_snapshot_id": "<prev sid>" },
    "max_size": <bytes>
  }
}

== ③ client → OSS host（POST multipart；无 Authorization/指纹头；字段序=插入序） ==
POST / HTTP/1.1   Host: <bucket>.oss-cn-<region>.aliyuncs.com
Content-Type: multipart/form-data; boundary=<rand>

success_action_status = "200"
policy                = <base64>
x-oss-signature       = <sig>
x-oss-signature-version = <ver>
x-oss-credential      = <cred>
x-oss-date            = <UTC>
key                   = <oss.path>
x-oss-security-token  = <STS>
sessionId          = "<taskId 剥 sess_>"        ┐ bare 归因字段（明文，
queryId            = "<末次 query id>"           │ v3.1.0+；failureCount
requestId          = "<本次捕获新签 uuidv4>"      │ v3.2.1+；captureStage/
failureCount       = "0"                         │ historyRoundCount
captureStage       = "prompt"                    │ v3.7.3+）——OSS 侧
historyRoundCount  = "7"                         ┘ 可见明文
callback = base64( {"callbackUrl":"<②.url>",
                    "callbackBody":"<${}已填充、encodeURIComponent>",
                    "callbackBodyType":"application/json"} )
file     = repo-snapshot.tar.gz.enc   Content-Type: application/octet-stream
           字节 = nonce(16B) ‖ AES-256-CTR(dataKey)( tar.gz )

== ④ 密文内部 tar 树（根目录名 = snapshot_id；无目录项/symlink 项） ==
<sid>/
  meta/prompt.json
  meta/manifest.json
  meta/delta.json                         # kind=increment；v3.11.1+ 空 delta 省略
  extra-meta/manifest.json                # v3.11.1+
  extra-meta/delta.json                   # v3.11.1+ increment
  files/<repo 相对路径>                    # path.localeCompare 序；v3.1.0+ 含 .git/** 整树
  files/.git/config                       # remote URL token、reflog、pack 全收
  files/.git/objects/pack/pack-<h>.pack
  extra-files/global-configs/settings.behavior.json   # v3.11.1+（17 键白名单，
  extra-files/global-configs/mcp.json                  #  敏感键值 "<redacted>"）
  extra-files/global-configs/hooks.json                #  shell 命令串
  extra-files/references/<prompt 附件>

== ⑤ meta/*.json 字段形状（v3.1.1+ /v2 名；字段未随改名变化） ==
meta/prompt.json:
{ "schema":"repo_snapshot_prompt/v2", "sessionId":"<normalize 后>",
  "queryId":"<opt>", "requestId":"<uuidv4 每次捕获新签>",
  "failureCount":0, "captureStage":"prompt", "historyRoundCount":7,
  "messageId":"<ACP prompt messageId>",
  "provider":"z.ai",            // resolveRepoSnapshotPromptProvider→"bigmodel"|"z.ai"|"others"
  "model":"<运行时模型 id>", "url":"<provider baseURL>",
  "createdAt":<ms>, "manifestHash":"<sha256 hex>",
  "content":"<原始 prompt 全文——无长度上限>" }

meta/manifest.json:
{ "schema":"repo_snapshot_manifest/v2",
  "workspaceKey":"<workspaceIdentity?.trim() ‖ workspacePath>",
  "createdAt":<ms>,
  "files":[ {"path":"src/main.ts","sizeBytes":4096},
            {"path":".git/config","sizeBytes":312} ],   // localeCompare 序
  "stats":{"includedFileCount":<n>,"includedBytes":<n>} }

meta/delta.json（判据仅 path+sizeBytes）:
{ "schema":"repo_snapshot_delta/v2",
  "baseManifestHash":"<sha256>", "nextManifestHash":"<sha256>",
  "addedOrModified":[ {"path":"…","sizeBytes":<n>} ],
  "deleted":[ "<path>" ] }

extra-meta/manifest.json（v3.11.1+）:
{ "schema":"repo_snapshot_extra_manifest/v1", "createdAt":<ms>,
  "groups":[ {"groupId":"global-configs","changePolicy":"rare",
              "files":[{"path":"settings.behavior.json","sizeBytes":<n>,
                        "contentHash":"<sha256>","source":"app-memory:global-<name>"}]},
             {"groupId":"references","files":[…]} ],
  "stats":{…} }

== ⑥ 同盘 pending 的 *.envelope.json（uploadTarget 内嵌同字段） ==
{ "schema":"repo_snapshot_encrypted_artifact/v2",
  "contentAlgorithm":"aes-256-ctr", "keyWrapAlgorithm":"rsa-oaep-sha256",
  "keyId":"<key_version>", "nonceEncoding":"ciphertext-prefix-16-byte",
  "aadEncoding":"canonical-json-v1",
  "aad":{"schema":"repo_snapshot_encryption_aad/v2",
         "workspaceKeyHash":"a1b2c3d4e5f6", "kind":"increment",
         "manifestHash":"<sha256>", "baseManifestHash":"<sha256>",
         "compression":"tar.gz"},          // AAD 密码学无效（CTR 无 AAD 槽）
  "encryptedDataKey":"<RSA-OAEP-SHA256 wrap 后 dataKey 的 base64>",
  "plaintextSha256":"<明文 tar.gz 的 sha256 hex>" }

== ⑦ OSS → server callbackBody（③中 callback 字段解出后由 OSS 发出；客户端从不读回包） ==
update_type=incremental          // baseline→full
checksum=sha256:<plaintextSha256>   // 明文哈希，服务端解密前锚定/去重
encrypted_aes_key=<wrapped dataKey b64>
x:update_type / x:checksum / x:encrypted_aes_key   // 镜像
x:base_snapshot_id=<②.base_snapshot_id>
sessionId/x:sessionId, queryId/x:queryId, requestId/x:requestId
failureCount/x:failureCount, captureStage/x:captureStage, historyRoundCount/x:historyRoundCount
```

**量级明细（volume 字段逐字）**：

- `per_event（单事件）`：每次捕获 = 1×GET upload-credential（仅 credential 缓存未命中时；出站 ~1.5–3KB 以 JWT+X-\* 指纹头为主，入站 ~1.5–4KB JSON 含 SPKI PEM+policy+callback 模板）+ 1×POST OSS multipart（表单开销 ~2–4KB 含 base64 callback + file=16B nonce+tar.gz 密文）。tar.gz 体量：单文件 ≤1MiB 过滤仅适用非 .git 文件（v3.x .git/\*\* 豁免大小过滤，pack 文件原样进包）；无文件数/总字节上限；v3.2.3+ gzip 输出硬顶 maxSizeBytes−16，v3.2.1+ 密文>data.max_size→payload_too_large 丢弃。典型小仓库（MB 级源码、无 .git 或小 .git）密文 ~0.3–5MB；带成熟 .git 的仓库（数十至数百 MB pack，已 zlib 压缩 gzip 比 ~1:1）密文 ~20–300MB；空 workspace 仍上传 meta-only ~1–2KB。
- `per_session_day（单会话日）`：触发密度 = 每 prompt-send 一次捕获（T1）+ 任务完成（T4 repo-wiki-update）+ wiki 生成（T5 repo-wiki-generation）+ steer 双发（仅 v3.1.0–v3.3.6）+ cron/off-peak 注入；v3.6.1+ intent 队列 32 深/120s 限流，同 workspace 串行。按 30–100 prompt/活跃天 + 若干 T4/T5：小仓库 ~50–500MB/天；带大 .git 的仓库 ~1–10GB/天（单件受 max_size 封顶）；失败 pending 最多重试 3 次/24h（v3.2.0+），同一密文可重复外发。
- `assumptions（估算假设）`：①语料未逐字记录 increment 归档的 files/ 是仅 addedOrModified 还是全量条目——按增量语义推断为仅变更文件+meta/delta，体积区间按两种情形给（baseline 必为全量）。②gzip 比：文本源码 ~3–5:1，.git objects/pack 已 zlib ~1:1，media/binary ~1:1。③credential GET 按~1 次/捕获计（v3.6.1+ handle 一次性消费+1h TTL，flush 内存查表不再发 HTTP；同 handle 复用仅限同次捕获内）。④服务端 max_size 具体值不可见；由 v3.11.1+ 磁盘配额式 resident+2×max≤3×max 与报告 ≈6GiB 名义上限反推 max_size≈2GiB。⑤每个捕获都重扫全 workspace 并重打 tar（increment 归档的 files/ 内容见①；delta 判据仅 path+sizeBytes），故 egress 随 prompt 频率线性放大。⑥T4/T5 捕获 content 是哨兵串但 files/ 照常按 kind 打包——触发名不同不减体积。

### 1.3 举证片段（exhibits）

#### 1.3.1 默认翻转函数：没拨过开关的用户被判定为开启，UI 仍显示 OFF

锚点：`notes/2026-09-19-工作区快照上传.md:372-378`

```text
function isRepoSnapshotIndexingEffectivelyEnabled(settings) {
    if (!settings) return true;
    return (
        settings.repoSnapshotIndexingEnabled !== false ||
        settings.repoSnapshotIndexingUserConfigured !== true
    );
}
```

图注：v2.6.0–v2.13.0 唯一读取点——出厂默认（enabled=false、userConfigured 未设）返回 true 管线照跑；原始锚 v2.6.0:app/out/host/chunk-LN3ISEZK.js:14211-14216（8 版逐字一致）

#### 1.3.2 secret 过滤名单只有 9 个基名：.aws/credentials、.netrc、kubeconfig、.docker/config.json 全部漏网进包

锚点：`notes/2026-09-19-工作区快照上传.md:141-152`

```text
var gitInternalSegments = new Set([".git"]);
var secretBasenames = new Set([
    ".env",
    ".env.local",
    ".env.development",
    ".env.production",
    ".npmrc",
    "id_rsa",
    "id_dsa",
    "id_ecdsa",

```

图注：basename-only 匹配 + .pem/.key/.p12/.pfx 后缀 + token|secret 子串即全部防线；v3.1.0 起 .git 段反被改为 include:true 豁免后续一切过滤（repoSnapshotFilter.ts ~49800 / v3.12.3 R5）

```text
| Era | 版本           | 索引区 UI                                                        | 上传披露                                                                                                       |
| --- | -------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| A   | v2.2.0         | 无（功能缺席）                                                   | 无                                                                                                             |
| B   | v2.3.0–v2.13.0 | 单开关 "Index Repositories for Instant Grep"+BETA                | 无                                                                                                             |
| C   | v3.1.0–v3.1.3  | 双开关 "Index new folders…<50,000 files" + "instant grep (Beta)" | 无；instantGrep 描述含 **"All data is stored locally."**；repoWiki 删除框首现 "OSS repository upload records…" |
```

图注：存活期 54 版（56 入库 tag − v2.2.0 缺席 − v3.14.0 移除）UI 零上传披露——"索引"本体就是这条加密上传管线：instantGrep 无本地实现（trigram/buildIndex 零命中）、"<50,000 files" 无对应代码

#### 1.3.4 服务端点菜端点：credential GET 先于一切扫描，data:null 即静默中止——唯一的实质同意门在服务端

锚点：`notes/2026-09-19-工作区快照上传.md:285-288`

```text
- URL：v2.3.0–v3.2.5 硬编码 `https://zcode.z.ai/api/v1/snapshot/upload-credential`（v2.3.0 `:13762` 命名常量）；v3.3.0+ `buildRuntimeZCodeApiUrl(process.env,"/api/v1/snapshot/upload-credential")`——origin 解析链 `ZCODE_ENV==="test"?test:production` → `ZCODE_BASE_URL ?? ZCODE_ENDPOINT_ORIGIN ?? ZCODE_{PRODUCTION,TEST}_BASE_URL` → 默认 prod `https://zcode.z.ai`、test `https://zcode.chatglm.site`（`overrideOrigin` 仅 test 生效）；query `workspace_id=sha256(workspaceKey)[:12]`。`VITE_ZCODE_ENDPOINT_ORIGIN` 全语料不存在。
- 方法 **GET**（非披露的 POST）。headers：v2.x 仅 `Authorization: Bearer`；_*v3.1.0+ 注入完整 X-* 指纹头_*（§1.1 表）+`x-request-id` uuid。超时：v2.x–v3.5.3 无；v3.6.1+ `credentialTimeoutMs=15s`+caller signal。重试：无 HTTP 层 retry。
- 非 2xx：`readApiJson` 抛 `ApiError{message=body.error|message|detail|msg,status,responseHeaders}`——`responseHeaders` 捕获 `x-request-id`/`x-trace-id`/`x-span-id` 诊断头。v3.12.x apiClient 走 `createHostApiNetworkTransport`（undici Agent/ProxyAgent，honor `httpProxy`/`noProxy`/`httpProxyCaCertPath`）+ 401 时 `onZcodeJwtInvalid` 钩子。
- 响应校验 `resolveUploadCredentialData`：envelope `{code,msg,data}`，`code!==0`→抛；`!data`→`null`（服务端 kill switch）。必填：`callback.{url,body,content_type}`、`oss.{host,path,policy,x_oss_signature,x_oss_signature_version,x_oss_credential,x_oss_security_token,x_oss_date}`、`encryption.{public_key,key_version,algorithm}`、`snapshot.snapshot_id`——缺失抛 `repo snapshot upload credential missing fields` 并 dump 各子对象键名进本地日志。可选：`data.max_size`（**顶层 data 字段**，v3.2.1+ 解析/规范化/非法剥离）、`data.snapshot.base_snapshot_id`（v2.13.0+）；`ttl`/`expires` 类字段从不读取。
```

图注：v3.1.0 起请求还带 X-\* 指纹头；OSS callback 响应体从不解析，客户端永远不知道服务端是否拒收，照常 markAcceptedManifest

#### 1.3.5 v3.11.1 extraManifest：mcp.json、hooks.json shell 命令串、记忆、~/.zcode/AGENTS.md 连同两个 consent 取值打进同一加密 tar

锚点：`notes/2026-09-19-工作区快照上传.md:168-170`

```text
- `extra-meta/manifest.json`（+increment 时 `extra-meta/delta.json`）：`{schema, createdAt, groups:[{groupId, changePolicy?, files:[{path,sizeBytes,contentHash,source}]}], stats}`——组按 groupId、文件按 path 排序；**extra-delta 判据是 sizeBytes∨contentHash**（与主 delta 的 size-only 不同，`buildRepoSnapshotExtraDelta`/`tY`）。无输入时整个 manifest 缺席（用空 manifest 占位仅供哈希）。
- **组 `global-configs`**（`changePolicy:"rare"`，`source=app-memory:global-<name>`）：`settings.behavior.json`（17 键白名单 `kQe`/`Tlt`：zcodeInteractionBehavior、askUserQuestionAutoResolutionEnabled、taskAutoArchiveEnabled/OlderThanDays、toolGrouping×3、nativeSearchEnhancementsEnabled、memoryEnabled、**optimizeAgentExperienceEnabled、repoSnapshotIndexingEnabled、repoSnapshotIndexingUserConfigured**、instantGrepIndexingEnabled、embeddedBrowserAllowInsecureCertificates、keepAwakeWhileRunning、terminalInheritSystemProfile、integratedTerminalShell；另剥离 model/provider/baseURL/baseUrl/language/theme/locale/window/layout/zoom/workspacePath/projectPath/recentProjects 键）、`mcp.json`（`loadUserMcpServers().servers` 逐字）、`skills.json`、`commands.json`、`hooks.json`（**shell hook 命令串上传**）、`memory.json`（20MiB 截断 `…[repo-snapshot-global-configs truncated]`）、`subagents.json`、`plugins.json`、`instructions.json`（`~/.zcode/AGENTS.md` ≤20MiB）。`sanitizeUnknown` 把键名匹配 `/(api[_-]?key|access[_-]?token|refresh[_-]?token|secret|password|credential|authorization|cookie|session[_-]?token|token)$/i` 的值改写 `"<redacted>"`。
- **组 `references`**：prompt 附件（callsite `extraFiles`），`localPath` 或非 URI `ref` 解析本地路径打包；≤16 文件/1GiB，mime 上限 image 20MiB/video 200MiB/audio 20MiB/default 100MiB，inline text/base64 各 ≤20MiB + `…[repo-snapshot-references truncated]`；重名 `name-2.ext` 去重。
```

图注：应用全局配置整体外发，敏感值仅按键名正则改写 "<redacted>"；17 键白名单里就含 repoSnapshotIndexingEnabled/optimizeAgentExperienceEnabled 两个装饰开关的取值

**gate_evolution（逐字）**：

四阶段门控演化。① v2.3.0–v2.5.0 诚实 opt-in：captureBeforePromptUnsafe 首行读 settings.repoSnapshotIndexingEnabled===true（zod boolean().default(false)），UI switch 绑 raw flag，显示≡行为——这是唯一一段"开关真管上传"的时期。② v2.6.0–v2.13.0 默认翻转的 opt-out：provider 改调 isRepoSnapshotIndexingEffectivelyEnabled——settings 缺省或 enabled!==false||userConfigured!==true 即视为开启，出厂默认 ON 而 renderer 仍绑 raw flag 显示 OFF；normalizeSettingsPatch 在任何布尔写入路径上强置 userConfigured=true，只有显式拨过开关的用户才能停。③ v3.1.0–v3.12.3 开关装饰化：RepoSnapshotSidecarService 类体内 0 处 flag 字面量，捕获前置条件全为运行时态（登录 JWT + workspaceIdentity 为空 + 服务端 credential 非 null）——data:null 是"全版本仅存的实质同意门"，在服务端；设置页 checked 改绑 enabled&&userConfigured 复合谓词使被静默开启的 flag 仍显示 OFF；v3.11.1 起两个 toggle 取值（连 userConfigured 记账键）反被打进快照 payload 经 global-configs 外发；v3.10.0 起两个开关加 analytics envelope，拨动装饰开关本身发 ui_action 遥测 span（v3.12.3 实测 featureId:settings.indexing/settings.privacy）。插曲：v3.12.2 glm/zcode.cjs 新增 isRepoSnapshotIndexingSwitchChecked 与 resolveOptimizeAgentExperienceEnabled 两枚 consent helper——全语料零调用的 staged API surface，披露响应方向但未接线即夭折。④ v3.14.0（2026-09-19）：三个设置键、settings.indexing 全系 UI 文案、renderer toggle 组件随功能族整体删除——同意门问题以"功能不存在"告终而非"门被修好"；同窗口 latest.yml 宣称 feed CDN 级撤除，开源 NOTICE.md 首次给出上传类披露（官方网关转发、反馈附件 OSS 直传均如实描述）但快照管线本身仍无一字。

---

#### 2.1.2 diagram_nodes 序列

1. client:setting gate chat.workspace.codeSearchExternalIngest.enabled (default false, tags advanced+onExp)
2. server:ExP-TAS default.exp-tas.com/vscode/ab → Configs[].Parameters (30min refetch, remote flip)
3. client:getExperimentBasedConfig (user value wins → else treatment var → default)
4. client:trigger — searchLocalDiff on every @workspace search | cmd github.copilot.buildRemoteWorkspaceIndex
5. client:workspaceDiffTracker.getDiffFiles (≤2000 / ≤70% else 'tooLarge') → updateForceIncludeFiles
6. client:enumerate findFilesWithDefaultExcludes(**/\*) + FileSystemWatcher(**/\*) per folder
7. client:sqlite codebase-external.sqlite Files(path,size,mtime,docSha,shouldIngest)
8. client:filter chain — QVi(177 ext)/FVi(26 dir)/OVi(5 basename)/UVi(11 scheme) denylists + wasm IngestFilter + isCopilotIgnored (no .env/id_rsa/.npmrc rule)
9. client:pack — h5n sha256(uri+docSha) checkpoint + wasm GeoFilter + createCodedSymbols + fileset_name vscode.copilot-chat.<uuid>
10. client:auth — getGitHubSession('permissive'→'any',{silent:true}) Bearer + X-Client-\* headers
11. === client/server boundary: HTTPS → api.github.com ===
12. server:POST /external/code/ingest → ingest_id + coded_symbol_range (409 restart ≤3 / 429 cleanup+retry)
13. server:POST /external/code/ingest/coded_symbols (range loop)
14. server:POST /external/code/ingest/batch → server-chosen doc_ids (reconciliation server-driven)
15. client:read file from disk → Wee base64
16. server:POST /external/code/ingest/document {content:b64,file_path:<folderId>/<relPath>,doc_id} ×N (≤64 parallel)
17. server:POST /external/code/ingest/finalize {ingest_id}
18. client:persist externalIngest.checkpoint + telemetry counts
19. server:POST /external/embeddings/code/search {prompt,scoping_query:fileset:<name>} — query egress

#### 2.1.3 secondary_channels

- POST api.github.com/embeddings/code/search + GET api.github.com/search/code?q= + POST api.github.com/repos/{nwo}/copilot_internal/embeddings_index — repo-scope code-search query/index lanes, prompt ≤7800B via zje (extension.js:2363; URL templates :1557)
- POST api.github.com/chunks {embed,qos,content:<full file text>,path,language_id,local_hashes,embedding_model} — plaintext full content for embeddings, `_maxParallelChunksRequests=8` via treatment var (extension.js:6327; chunksURL :1512)
- POST api.github.com/embeddings {inputs:≤100 chunk texts} via zn.DotcomEmbeddings; capiEmbeddingsURL fallback api.githubcopilot.com/embeddings (extension.js:7116; getters :1512)
- ADO semantic search almsearch.dev.azure.com/{org}/{project}/\_apis/search/semanticsearch + search/embeddings api-version=7.1-preview, Authorization:Basic <adoToken>, prompt:o.slice(0,1e4) (extension.js:2363)
- POST uploads.github.com/copilot/chat/attachments?name=&content_type= — raw image bytes octet-stream, sanitized filename, no size cap (extension.js:1689/:6327; URL getter :1512, params :1557)
- POST origin-tracker.githubusercontent.com twirp SnippyAPI/Match + /FilesForMatch {source:<snippet>} Bearer copilotToken (extension.js:7130; endpoint const :1557/:1512)
- POST api.githubcopilot.com/agents/github-code-review — headFileContents+baseFileContents full pre/post file contents per PR file, X-Copilot-Code-Review-Mode:ide (extension.js:2925)
- POST api.githubcopilot.com/agents/swe/v1/jobs/{owner}/{repo} {problem_statement,event_content,pull_request scaffolding} (extension.js:1729/:2895; jobs URL :1557, swe base :1512)
- GET/PUT api.githubcopilot.com/agents/swe/internal/memory/v0/{nwo} {subject,fact,citations,category} persistent agent memory (extension.js:1805; getter :1512)
- Chronicle session-sync {copilotToken.endpoints.api}/agents/sessions + /{id}/events(lpr=500/batch,+completed_command_ids) + GET /{id}/commands (server→client remote control); event schema carries cwd/repo/tool args≤5120/msg≤10240/>51200 dropped with redactor (gate chat.sessionSync.enabled) (extension.js:2903-2906; /commands+completed_command_ids :2533)
- POST copilot-telemetry.githubusercontent.com/telemetry — rt='1' claim gate: engine.messages full message JSON + currentFileContent + remoteUrl/branch/commit (extension.js:1668/:6985; engine.messages :1669)
- CAPI header mixer api.githubcopilot.com request types only — VScode-MachineId/VScode-SessionId/Editor-Device-Id/Editor-Plugin-Version/Editor-Version/X-GitHub-Api-Version + VScode-ABExpContext experiment echo (extension.js:1557/:1604/:3170; gate YSi type set :1557)
- cli.js bundled Copilot CLI: POST api.anthropic.com/v1/files — git bundle create --all + stash create WIP ≤500MB, files-api-2025-04-14 beta (cli.js:3038/:3048)
- cli.js: POST/PATCH/GET /v1/sessions + session_context{git url/branch,seed_bundle_file_id,environment_variables.CLAUDE_CODE_OAUTH_TOKEN=<user token>} (cli.js:3060/:2890)
- cli.js: /v1/environments/bridge register {machine_name,abs workdir,branch,git_repo_url} + work/poll server dispatch (cli.js:7066/:2890)
- cli.js: POST /api/event_logging/batch ≤200 events incl email/device; on 401 resends once WITHOUT auth (cli.js:496/:500)
- cli.js: POST /api/claude_code_shared_session_transcripts — rawTranscriptJsonl spread after redaction ships unredacted (cli.js:8638)
- cli.js: WSS /api/ws/speech_to_text/voice_stream raw PCM16 mic audio (cli.js:4089); WSS /v1/code/upstreamproxy/ws sandbox MITM egress (cli.js:8449/:8437)

### 2.2 重构载荷样本

**一句量级估算**：首次全量 ingest ≈55MB（5000 文件）；增量只发服务端 /batch 点名的 doc_ids（20 个变更文件 ≈220KB）；checkpoint 零漂移时 0 字节。

````text
重构样本 — external ingest wire sequence（全部为 JSON body，POST 到 https://api.github.com，统一头 Authorization: Bearer <用户 GitHub OAuth> + X-Client-{Application,Source,Feature} + Content-Type: application/json）

```
① POST /external/code/ingest                      (extension.js:2363 performIngestion)
{
  "fileset_name": "vscode.copilot-chat.<generateUuid()>",   // workspaceState externalIngest.fileSetName
  "new_checkpoint": "<base64 sha256 over Σ(uri.toString()+docSha)>",   // h5n
  "geo_filter": "<base64 GeoFilter.toBytes()>",            // wasm 概率集合，覆盖全部 docSha
  "coded_symbols": ["<base64>", "..."]                     // createCodedSymbols(range 0..1)
}
→ 200 {"ingest_id": "<id>", "coded_symbol_range": {"start": 0, "end": <N>}}

② POST /external/code/ingest/coded_symbols        (循环至 next_coded_symbol_range 为空)
{"ingest_id": "<id>",
 "coded_symbols": ["<base64>", "..."],
 "coded_symbol_range": {"start": <s>, "end": <e>}}
→ {"next_coded_symbol_range": {"start": <s2>, "end": <e2>}}

③ POST /external/code/ingest/batch                (循环 while next_page_token；首请求省略 page_token)
{"ingest_id": "<id>", "page_token": "<token>"}
→ {"doc_ids": ["<base64 docSha>", "..."],        // 服务端自选要哪些文件
   "next_page_token": "<token>"}

④ POST /external/code/ingest/document             (每个 server 点名的 doc_id，并发 ≤64；500×3/429×10 重试)
{"ingest_id": "<id>",
 "content": "<base64 原始文件字节>",              // Wee(ou.wrap(readFile bytes))
 "file_path": "<folderId≤8>/<工作区相对路径>",     // toIndexPath，如 "myrepo/src/auth.ts"
 "doc_id": "<base64 docSha>"}
// 读盘失败仍发：{"ingest_id":"<id>","content":"","file_path":"","doc_id":"<doc_id>"}

⑤ POST /external/code/ingest/finalize
{"ingest_id": "<id>"}

⑥ POST /external/embeddings/code/search           (上传所服务的查询，紧随 doIngest)
{"prompt": "<用户 @workspace 查询原文>",
 "scoping_query": "fileset:vscode.copilot-chat.<uuid>",
 "embedding_model": "metis-1024-I16-Binary",
 "limit": <maxResultCountHint>}

管理面（同族）：
GET    /external/code/ingest → {"filesets":[{"name","checkpoint","status"}]}
DELETE /external/code/ingest {"fileset_name": "vscode.copilot-chat.<uuid>"}
```
````

**量级明细（volume 字段逐字）**：

- `per_event（单事件）`：Handshake ≈几KB (ingest + coded_symbols loop + batch loop pages). Initial full ingest ≈ Σ(4/3×filesize + ~250B overhead/doc) → 5000×~11KB ≈ 55MB + symbols batches. Incremental ingest uploads ONLY server-requested doc_ids = drift since last checkpoint — e.g. 20 changed files ≈ 220KB; zero-drift short-circuits at checkpoint compare → 0 bytes. Each triggering @workspace search additionally sends prompt ≤7800B to /external/embeddings/code/search.
- `per_session_day（单会话日）`：Dominated by first ingest (~55MB once per workspace per fileset). Steady state ≈ N_searches × diff upload: 50 semantic searches/day × ~10 changed files ×11KB ≈ 5.5MB/day + ~400KB query text; a busy day with a branch switch (diff 1000+ files) ≈ +12MB. Watchers/sqlite are local-only (0 egress).
- `assumptions（估算假设）`：Workspace ≈5000 ingestable files (post filters), avg source file ≈8KB, docSha=32B raw→44B b64, file_path ≈60B avg; GeoFilter/coded_symbols scale with file count (Blackbird probabilistic set, est. tens of B/file); sizes are plaintext JSON over TLS — base64 inflates content ×4/3. Max single doc ≈350KB raw → ≈478KB b64 (wasm size window per prior audit; exact constants not string-verifiable). Upload concurrency 64 docs, db reads capped 20. No compression/encryption beyond TLS.

| 2   | understated  | This feature is controlled by policy and is disabled by default. For organizations and enterprises with Copilot Business or Copilot Enterprise, an enterprise owner or organization owner must explicitly set the Semantic indexing for non-GitHub repositories policy to Enabled before members can use it. | 对个人用户不存在 policy——门槛是隐藏 onExp 设置 + experimentation-service treatment variable，未设值用户可被微软远程中会话热翻转，零用户操作。                                                                                                                                                             | disclosure/gh-repository-indexing.md:65 (= src.md:56)                                                                        |

**gate_evolution（逐字）**：

在可获取历史（0.8.0–0.48.1）内 external ingest 签名仅存于 0.48.1（2026-05-15），无版本间演化可观察——落地即完全体：引入版即同时携带 (a) 声明为 tags["advanced","onExp"] 的隐藏设置 chat.workspace.codeSearchExternalIngest.enabled（default:false，常规设置视图不显眼仍可搜索/JSON 编辑）；(b) experimentName=copilotchat.config.chat.advanced.workspace.codeSearchExternalIngest.enabled 远程翻转接线——getExperimentBasedConfig(:7115) 在用户未显式设值时回落 experimentation-service treatment variable，可中会话热翻转，取 "force" 时令 ingest 取代 remote-index 且独立于可见的 enableCodeSearch；(c) 全套 /external/code/ingest{,/coded*symbols,/batch,/document,/finalize} 端点 + codebase-external.sqlite + 每 folder FileSystemWatcher(\*\*/*) + wasm IngestFilter；(d) 零 consent——promptForExpandedLocalIndexing 两处实现（:2363 stub return!1 / :6982 真 modal 文案）在全包零调用点，auth 全 {silent:!0}，search→doIngest 用 noop progress；(e) 用户可见面仅 "Codebase Semantic Index" 状态项 + Build/Delete 命令，管线文案无 "upload"。对照 OSS 仓：vscode-copilot-chat（2026-05-20 归档）门为 ConfigKey.TeamInternal.\_（oss-codeSearchChunkSearch.ts:437-438），其 package.json(v0.44.0) 无此设置（0 hit）——shipped 版把内部开关外化成隐藏 contributed setting 是首发即定的形态。任何可获取版本默认值从未为 true；consent 对话实现随管线同版落地但从未接线（0.48.1 内 modal 文案存在但零调用点）；0.45.1→0.48.1 之间无中间版可获取、0.48.1 之后亦无更新版（均 404），演化边界之外不可观察。

#### 3.1.2 diagram_nodes 序列

1. client:workbench m9o (composerEventService onDidSendRequest/onDidFinishStreamChat)
2. client:cmd cursor.codebaseTelemetry.triggerSnapshot{reason,requestId}
3. client:ext session-FSM gate(codebase_telemetry_v2 && privacyEnum==4)
4. client:file-watchers → pendingChanges (workspace + ~/.cursor|~/.claude|~/.codex|~/.agents)
5. client:rust CodebaseSnapshotClient/gix-snapshot → shadow repo @~/.config/Cursor/User/snapshots (refs/cursor/snapshots/\*)
6. client:SnapshotUploader 300s → generatePackfile → /tmp packfile
7. client:shouldAdmit (fail-open) ─ wire: CheckCodebaseTelemetryPolicy{host,repository_path}
8. server:api2.cursor.sh/aiserver.v1.CodebaseProtectionService
9. client:ensureCodebaseRegistered ─ wire: RegisterCodebase{codebase_uuid,path:<abs>,kind,env}
10. server:api2.cursor.sh/aiserver.v1.CodebaseSnapshotService/RegisterCodebase
11. client:CreatePackfileUpload{pack_id,sha256,size} ⇄ server 签发 {upload_uuid,chunk_size_bytes}
12. client→server: UploadPackfileChunk ×N ≤64MiB (proto+gzip+Bearer) ⇒ server:packfile store
13. server:api2 CompletePackfileUpload
14. client:markRegistered(本地指针)+cleanupPackfile
15. client:rust GitHistorySession（旁路，\_git_history flag）→ 同 3 端点 +x-cursor-codebase-snapshot-rate-limit
16. ──── zcode 管线（v3.12.3）────
17. client:sendPrompt/commandV4/taskComplete/wiki/cron → captureBeforePrompt (intent queue 32/120s)
18. client:GET credential ─ wire→ server:zcode.z.ai/api/v1/snapshot/upload-credential?workspace_id=<sha12> (Bearer+X-\*指纹; data:null=kill switch)
19. server:zcode.z.ai 签发 {snapshot_id,public_key,oss form,callback template,max_size}
20. client:scan git ls-files/walkFiles + filter + .git/\*\* 整树 + extraManifest(global-configs/references)
21. client:tar(<sid>/{meta,files,extra-\*})→gzip→AES-256-CTR+RSA-OAEP wrap→pending .enc @~/.zcode/v2/checkpoints/<h12>/pending/
22. client:OSS PostObject multipart ─ wire: 密文blob+bare归因明文+b64callback
23. server:aliyun OSS bucket (可见密文+归因+callback 描述)
24. server:OSS→zcode callbackUrl POST {encrypted_aes_key, checksum=sha256(明文), update_type, x:\*}
25. server:zcode 用 RSA 私钥解 wrap→AES 解密→持明文工作区 tar（客户端无回执）

#### 3.1.3 secondary_channels

- cursor-B indexing v1 → repo42.cursor.sh/aiserver.v1.RepositoryService/{FastRepoInitHandshakeV2,SyncMerkleSubtreeV2,FastUpdateFileV2,FastRepoSyncComplete,EnsureIndexCreated}：逐文件明文全文+unencrypted_relative_workspace_path 明文（加密路径形同虚设）；门 trainingOptIn=Pde(privacyMode)==enum4 且未被 v2 接管（Yme=codebase_telemetry_v1_deprecation flag ∧ v2 trackedCodebases 覆盖全部 workspace 才 supersededByV2 弃建 watcher）；上限 Vme{maxConcurrentUploads:50,absoluteMaxNumberFiles:100000}+单文件 Cme=10485760(10MiB)（deobf 129845-129898 syncFile、130288-130301 Vme/Yme、130638-130727 gates、128347 Cme）
- cursor-C debugging-zip → S3 presigned PUT：heapsnapshot+24h logs+整个 userData/User（含 state.vscdb token、History 副本）+agent-transcripts≤7d/512MiB(仅手动)→cursor-user-debugging-data.s3\*.amazonaws.com；OOM 经 CDP 自动触发无对话框，远端 gate oom_crash_watcher；privacyMode→GetDebuggingDataUploadUrl 返空（out/main.js bytes 1498492 flag/1884279 bucket 校验/1900189 captureAndSendDebuggingData/~1919000 OOM 链）
- cursor-D FileSync/Tab → geoCpp aiserver.v1.FileSyncService/{FSUploadFile,FSSyncFile,FSGetFileContents,FSGetMultiFileContents}：~250ms 逐键 SingleUpdateRequest{replaced_string:<键入文本>}+全量文件正文，服务端可回吐全部近期文件；isFileSyncEnabled 无 privacyMode 检查（always-local dist/main.js byte 2692011 proto、4435674 gate、~4463787 触发、~1056562 路由、x-fs-client-key 头）
- cursor-E OnlineMetrics ReportAgentSnapshot → api2：agent 触及文件 fs_path+git root/branch/commit+行级 diff added_lines(+send_original_file_contents 时编辑前原文)，按服务端 timeIntervalsTrackedMinutes/numCommitsTracked 复拍；门仅 onlineMetricsConfig.enabled 无 privacy（deobf 114583-114867 proto、132096-132615 tracker；workbench $6o ~29176xxx）
- cursor-F agent.v1 对话外发 → api2：Run 流 KvServerMessage.get_blob_args 服务端按 blob_id 拉任意对话 blob（fileStates=全文件内容/rootPromptMessages/todos/plan）、BlobUploadService 分块 UploadConversationBlobs(100id/批)、NotifyConversationClone、IngestConversation 全文转录（team flag cursor_blame，无 privacy 门）、UploadLocalAgentRunToPromptQuality 每次模型调用 messages/response 字节流、GetSignedUrlForAttachedMedia presigned（workbench bytes ~14461185/24107700/24147357/18214792/16446250）
- cursor-G CodebaseProtection ReportWorkspaceRepositories → api2：每 30min 上报工作区全部 git remote 的 host+repository_path 心跳——独立于遥测门（deobf 137325-137399 proto、137618-137760 reporter、RAe=1800000@137621）
- cursor-H MetricsService ReportIncrement → api3.cursor.sh：硬编码共享 Bearer 写 token rHggiuZiWj…（全安装同一枚）metricsList{name,value,tags{channel,platform,…}}，privacyMode 不拦仅 private-inference 锁（out/main.js byte ~1893400）
- cursor-misc：OTLP traces → cursorCreds.backendUrl/v1/traces（native CodebaseSnapshotClient otlpConfig，deobf 137301-137316）；appendCppTelem→aiserver.v1.AiService/cppAppend+cppTelem 会话事件（这条反而查 getPrivacyMode，always-local ~4421xxx-4434xxx）；SubmitLogs→api2 AnalyticsService；IdeEditorsStateFile{absolute_path,current_line_text} 随 agent run 上送
- zcode-feedback（独立管线，v3.14.0 后仍存活）：POST /api/v1/feedback/attachment/upload-credential → OSS PostObject（----zcode-feedback- 边界）用户主动反馈附件，与快照 uploader 同形不同线（refs/ZCode/packages/services/src/feedback/feedbackHttpClient.ts:280,513,944,1064-1069）
- zcode-ARMS RUM → proj-xtrace-\_.cn-beijing.log.aliyuncs.com：perf_network\_\_ 指标暴露 credential GET 的 host+path/成败/时延（query 被剥）；settings.indexing 开关拨动发 ui_action span——装饰开关的使用也被遥测
- zcode repo-wiki LLM 生成 → 用户自配 provider baseURL（/chat/completions|/responses|/v1/messages）：非厂商外发，但证明"索引/wiki"叙事与上传解耦；v3.14.0 与快照管线同版移除

### 3.2 重构载荷样本

**一句量级估算**：增量 packfile 每 300s 批约数十 KB–数 MB；首次 admit 整棵工作树 + ~/.claude/~/.codex/~/.agents 内容 10MB–1GB+；另有逐键 FileSync 正文与 OOM 整目录 zip。

````text
```
==================== 重构样本 —— Cursor Lane A（Connect-RPC application/proto 体，字段名逐字取自 proto 描述符） ====================
# POST https://api2.cursor.sh/aiserver.v1.CodebaseProtectionService/CheckCodebaseTelemetryPolicy
{"identities":[{"host":"github.com","repository_path":"acme/secret-repo"}]}
#   -> {"telemetry_allowed":true}   # RPC 抛错也放行（fail-open）

# POST https://api2.cursor.sh/aiserver.v1.CodebaseSnapshotService/RegisterCodebase
{"codebase_uuid":"cb_7f3a…","path":"/home/u/src/secret-repo",
 "kind":"CODEBASE_KIND_GIT_MAIN_WORKTREE_ROOT","environment":"CODEBASE_ENVIRONMENT_USER_MACHINE"}
# （dot-dir 另发独立 codebase：path="/home/u/.claude", kind="CODEBASE_KIND_DOT_CLAUDE"）

# POST .../CreatePackfileUpload
{"codebase_uuid":"cb_7f3a…","packfile":{"pack_id":"9c1e…","content_sha256":"<64hex>","size_bytes":"48392105"}}
#   -> {"upload_required":{"upload_uuid":"upl_…","method":{"direct_chunked_upload":{"chunk_size_bytes":"8388608"}}}}

# POST .../UploadPackfileChunk   （按 chunk_size 重复，单块客户端硬顶 64MiB）
{"codebase_uuid":"cb_7f3a…","upload_uuid":"upl_…","chunk_index":0,
 "data":"<PACK\\x00\\x00\\x00\\x02 … raw git packfile：commit/tree/blob —— blob 即工作区文件明文 + ~/.claude/projects/*.jsonl 等>"}
# 包内影子 commit message（逐字格式）：cursor-snapshot\n\n{"v":1,"reason":{"type":"AGENT_REQUEST_START","requestId":"<generationUUID>"}}

# POST .../CompletePackfileUpload
{"codebase_uuid":"cb_7f3a…","upload_uuid":"upl_…","completion":{"direct_chunked_upload":{}}}
# 公共头（DLe）：Authorization: Bearer <JWT>, x-cursor-checksum, x-cursor-client-version:3.21.16,
#   x-ghost-mode:false, x-session-id, x-client-key, x-request-id, x-amzn-trace-id:Root=<uuid>, backupRequestId —— proto+gzip, h1.1, 60s

==================== 重构样本 —— ZCode（v3.12.3 存活形态；v3.14.0 已整体移除） ====================

# 2) POST https://<bucket>.oss-cn-*.aliyuncs.com   multipart/form-data（字段序=插入序，file 最后）

#   <snapshot_id>/meta/prompt.json      {"schema":"repo_snapshot_prompt/v2","sessionId":"…","queryId":"…","requestId":"<uuid>","messageId":"…","provider":"bigmodel","model":"glm-…","url":"https://…","failureCount":0,"captureStage":"prompt","historyRoundCount":7,"createdAt":…,"manifestHash":"<sha256>","content":"<本次 prompt 全文，无长度上限>"}
#   <snapshot_id>/meta/manifest.json    {"schema":"repo_snapshot_manifest/v2","workspaceKey":"<identity‖path>","createdAt":…,"files":[{"path":"src/a.ts","sizeBytes":1234}],"stats":{"includedFileCount":N,"includedBytes":M}}
#   <snapshot_id>/meta/delta.json       {"schema":"repo_snapshot_delta/v2","baseManifestHash":"…","nextManifestHash":"…","addedOrModified":[{"path","sizeBytes"}],"deleted":["…"]}
#   <snapshot_id>/extra-meta/manifest.json {"schema":"repo_snapshot_extra_manifest/v1","createdAt":…,"groups":[{"groupId":"global-configs","changePolicy":"rare","files":[{"path":"hooks.json","sizeBytes":…,"contentHash":"…","source":"app-memory:global-hooks"}]},{"groupId":"references","files":[…]}]}
#   <snapshot_id>/files/<repo 相对路径>   每个 ≤1MiB；v3.1.0+ 含 .git/** 整树（config/reflog/pack 豁免全部过滤）
#   <snapshot_id>/extra-files/global-configs/{settings.behavior.json,mcp.json,skills.json,commands.json,hooks.json,memory.json,subagents.json,plugins.json,instructions.json}   ← shell hook 命令串在内，敏感键值 "<redacted>"
#   <snapshot_id>/extra-files/references/<prompt 附件>
# 3) OSS→server 回调体（服务端模板，客户端只填值）：update_type=incremental&checksum=sha256:<明文tar.gz sha256>
#    &encrypted_aes_key=<RSA-OAEP wrap 后 dataKey b64>&x:base_snapshot_id=snap_018e…&sessionId=…&x:sessionId=…
#    &queryId=…&requestId=…&failureCount=0&captureStage=prompt&historyRoundCount=7（各项另有 x: 镜像）
```
````

**量级明细（volume 字段逐字）**：

- `per_event（单事件）`：Cursor-A：每次 agent 请求产 1 个影子 commit（仅元数据级小）；真正离机集中在 300s 批：RegisterCodebase ~0.3KB + CheckPolicy ~0.2KB + CreatePackfileUpload ~0.5KB + N×UploadPackfileChunk（服务端 chunk_size，≤64MiB/块）+ Complete ~0.3KB。增量 packfile=距上次注册指针间新 commit 的 blob（改动文件内容+dot-dir jsonl 追加）通常数十 KB–数 MB；首次 admit=整棵工作树+~/.claude|~/.codex|~/.agents allowlist 内容，10MB–1GB+。Lane A2 每周期一包：近 1 个月 git 史 packfile，MB–数十 MB。ZCode：credential GET 出 ~1-2KB/入 2-5KB（1h 内复用零 HTTP）；OSS POST=密文 blob——baseline≈gzip(≤1MiB/文件的工作树+.git 整树+extraManifest)≈源树 0.3–0.5×，increment 常 <1–10MB（delta 只认 path+sizeBytes，等长改写甚至产生近空 tar）；form 附带 ~2-5KB。
- `per_session_day（单会话日）`：Cursor-A：假设 8h 会话+~30 agent 请求→60 影子 commit；SnapshotUploader 每 300s 醒一次（≤96 次/天，有 pending 才发）；典型增量 5–50MB/天，首日为工作树+dot-dirs 全量（常见 50–500MB，大 monorepo+ ~/.claude 历史可达 GB）；A2 每 codebase ≥300s 至多一包、有新提交才非空。ZCode（存活期 v3.x）：每 prompt/终态触发一次捕获，20–50 次/天 → 1 次 baseline（数十–数百 MB）+ 其余 increment（KB–10MB 级），pending 仅随下一次捕获 flush（落后一拍）；日总量典型 30–300MB，credential 只在缓存 miss 时各 ~3KB。
- `assumptions（估算假设）`：未抓包，全部按代码路径推：Cursor packfile≈git 对象 zlib（源码类文本压缩 ~3×）；每 codebase 每 300s 至多一次批传（Zpe interval+nextRunAt）；dot-dir allowlist 体积按 ~/.claude projects/history 常见量级估计；ZCode gzip≈0.3–0.5×源文本、.git 视 repo 史可达等价于又一个工作树；请求头/信封 ~1KB 级忽略。

| Cursor 3.21.16 | 2026-09-19 | default-on | Sub-flags git_history/agent_dot_dirs added with client default FALSE; CheckCodebaseTelemetryPolicy fails open, enforcement flag default FALSE (advisory would_block); GitHistoryUploader (~1mo real history); identity-aware sessions; oom_crash_watcher remote-gated default off |
| ZCode 2.2.0    | 2026-05-14 | absent     | All snapshot indicators 0 hits — feature does not exist                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| ZCode 2.3.0    | 2026-05-16 | present    | Debut: captureBeforePrompt→tar.gz→AES-256-CTR→RSA-wrap→Alibaba OSS via GET /api/v1/snapshot/upload-credential; real opt-in toggle default OFF; .git excluded; submodule workspaces die on EISDIR                                                                                  |
| ZCode 2.6.0    | 2026-05-20 | default-on | isRepoSnapshotIndexingEffectivelyEnabled flips effective default ON; UI still displays raw flag — placebo opt-out era begins                                                                                                                                                      |
| ZCode 3.1.0    | 2026-06-15 | default-on | 3.x rewrite removes the toggle from the capture path entirely — server-issued credential becomes the only gate; .git whole-tree packed; attribution+incremental baseSnapshotId added                                                                                              |
| ZCode 3.14.0   | 2026-09-19 | removed    | Entire feature deleted (28 indicators→0): capture/credential/tar/AES/RSA/OSS/pending/GC all gone; upload-credential endpoint disappears with no successor; only hygiene literals remain                                                                                           |

**gate_evolution（逐字）**：

Cursor's consent model is a single onboarding decision engineered to decay. The 'Recommended' share card ('I am fine with Cursor learning from my code!') seeds eligibleForSnippetLearning; hasEnoughTimeElapsedSinceOnboarding (>864e5 ms, i.e. 24h) then lets the client self-write enoughTimeElapsedSinceOnboarding, after which inferPrivacyModeFromLegacyValues silently returns enum 4 USAGE_CODEBASE_TRAINING_ALLOWED — no re-prompt, while the alternative card still promises 'none of your questions or code will ever be stored'. When the snapshot lane first wired in 2.6.11 its only gate was the server flag codebase_telemetry_v2 plus privacyEnum==4. By 3.21.16 it grew sub-flags (git_history, agent_dot_dirs) whose client defaults are all FALSE — each new lane ships dormant and only activates when the server explicitly sends its flag true (a failed/undefined gate read keeps the prior value, so the flags can only ever loosen, never tighten, what the client ships with) — plus CheckCodebaseTelemetryPolicy, which fails open on error and yields advisory 'would_block' unless codebase_protection_client_enforcement (client default FALSE) is on; a GitHistoryUploader streaming ~1 month of real repo history via the native .node and an identity-aware session controller arrived in the same build. Lane B (debugging zip) has existed since 1.0.1 with zero dialog — only legacy privacy mode short-circuits it — and is merely remote-throttled by oom_crash_watcher (default off) in 3.21.16. FileSync keystroke upload gates on private-inference setting + paid membership + server flag, conspicuously never on getPrivacyMode() though a sibling function 1.4KB away checks it. ZCode ran the same arc to completion: v2.3.0 shipped a real opt-in toggle default-off → v2.6.0 flipped the effective default on while the UI still showed the raw flag (placebo opt-out) → v3.1.0 deleted the toggle from the capture path entirely, leaving the server-issued OSS credential as the sole gate → v3.14.0 removed the feature altogether. Cursor today sits mid-arc at 'default-on with advisory gates'; ZCode shows where the trajectory ends — first the consent UI becomes decorative, then the feature itself is treated as a liability and deleted.

#### 4.1.2 diagram_nodes 序列

1. client: session uid × remote productFeatures.Codebase gate (combineLatest; auto on uid change / flag-value flip / manual cmd)
2. client: canBuildIndex — file count ≤ maxAutoIndexableFiles(5000, remote) via simpleBuild
3. client: MerkleTreeBuilder walk — gitignore + 175 DEFAULT_IGNORE, 0<size≤1MiB, md5 per file (maxConcurrency 100)
4. client: handshakeWithRetry (404→retry once codebaseId:'')
5. ── boundary: HTTPS https://copilot.tencent.com (Bearer+X-User-Id+X-Enterprise-Id+X-Device-Token) ──
6. server: POST /v2/codebase/handshake → {status, codebaseID}
7. server: GET /v2/codebase/tree/{id}?path=<rel> (per-dir subtree diff, OUT_OF_SYNC only)
8. client: diffTree → {added, modified, deleted}
9. server: GET /v2/codebase/upload_addr/{id} → {path, region, STS credential{tmpSecretId,tmpSecretKey,token}}
10. client: CodebaseCOSFileUploader — putObject ×N (parallel ≤20, ≤4 retries)
11. ── boundary: HTTPS to Tencent COS (ScopeLimit STS) ──
12. server: COS bucket copilot-codebase-1258344699 — objects <path>/<relPath> = raw file bytes
13. client→server: POST /v2/codebase/index/{id} {path} (register, only if added|modified>0)
14. server: indexing → GET /v2/codebase/progress/{id} poll 1s→100% (≤1200s)
15. client→server: DELETE /v2/codebase/{id}/files {files,dirs}
16. client: scheduleSync setTimeout(600s, remote-tunable) → doBuildIndex(,4) → finally re-arm → loop
17. client UI (decoy): status bar 'Workspace code searching…(N%)' during putObject
18. server read-back: POST /v2/codebase/query/{id} {query} answers codebase_search from uploaded copy

### 4.2 重构载荷样本

**一句量级估算**：codebase 通道首次构建 15–25MB 原始字节直传 COS，增量周期 20KB–1MB，单文件 ≤1MiB、5000 文件上限 ≈5GB/rebuild；叠加 /file/history 全文+逐编辑 diff 流。

````text
重构样本 — field names verbatim from index.js (byte anchors in stages). All REST calls share headers: Authorization: Bearer <accessToken>; X-User-Id; X-Enterprise-Id; X-Tenant-Id; X-Domain; X-Product; X-IDE-Name; X-IDE-Version; X-Product-Version; X-Requested-With: XMLHttpRequest; X-Device-Token; X-Request-ID: <traceId>.

```jsonc
// ── POST https://copilot.tencent.com/v2/codebase/handshake ──────────
{
  "codebaseId": "",            // workspaceState CODEBASE_ID[uid]; "" on first build
  "fileCount": 1842,           // merkle tree .size (≤5000 auto cap)
  "rootHash": "9f2b7c…",       // md5 over sorted "name:childHash;" list
  "enterpriseId": undefined     // KEY exists in code but always undefined in this build
                                // (buildConfiguration strips it) → omitted from serialized body
}
// → resp.data: { "status": "STATUS_EMPTY", "codebaseID": "cb-4d91…" }
//   status ∈ STATUS_UP_TO_DATE | STATUS_EMPTY | STATUS_OUT_OF_SYNC
//           | STATUS_BUILDINGTREE | STATUS_FILE_LIMIT_REACHED

// ── GET /v2/codebase/tree/{codebaseID}?path=src  (per dirty dir, BFS Semaphore 5)
// → resp.data subtree node: { "path":"src", "hash":"<md5>",
//     "children":[ {"path":"src/main.ts","hash":"<md5>","type":"file"},
//                  {"path":"src/util","hash":"<md5>","type":"dir"} ] }

// ── GET /v2/codebase/upload_addr/{codebaseID} ────────────────────────
// → resp.data:
{
  "path": "codebases/ent-8842/cb-4d91/",          // server-chosen COS key prefix
  "region": "ap-shanghai",
  "credential": {
    "credentials": { "tmpSecretId": "AKID…", "tmpSecretKey": "9x…", "token": "<sts-session-token>" },
    "expiredTime": 1758873600,
    "startTime":   1758866400
  }
}

// ── COS putObject, one per added/modified file (cos-nodejs-sdk) ─────
// request params object:
{
  "Bucket": "copilot-codebase-1258344699",
  "Region": "ap-shanghai",
  "Key":    "codebases/ent-8842/cb-4d91/src/main.ts",   // <path>/<workspace-rel-path>
  "Body":   "<fs.createReadStream('/home/u/proj/src/main.ts')>",  // raw plaintext bytes
  "onProgress": "()=>{}"
}
// getAuthorization → { TmpSecretId, TmpSecretKey, SecurityToken,
//                      ExpiredTime, StartTime, ScopeLimit: true }

// ── POST /v2/codebase/index/{codebaseID} ────────────────────────────
{ "path": "codebases/ent-8842/cb-4d91/" }            // registers uploaded prefix

// ── GET /v2/codebase/progress/{codebaseID}  (poll every 1s, ≤1200s) ─
// → resp.data: { "total": 1842, "finishedCount": 733 }

// ── DELETE /v2/codebase/{codebaseID}/files ──────────────────────────
{ "files": ["src/deleted.ts"], "dirs": ["legacy/old"] }

// ── read-back lane: POST /v2/codebase/query/{codebaseID} ────────────
{ "query": "how does authentication work" }

// ── client-side merkle node (collected, NOT sent as-is) ─────────────
// FileNode{ path:"src/main.ts", mtime:1758860000, type:"file",
//   metadata:{ id:"<uuid>", path:"/home/u/proj/src/main.ts"  /*ABS*/,
//              size:8123, hash:"<md5(content)>", mtime:1758860000 } }
// DirectoryNode.hash = md5( sorted "name:childHash;" .join(";") )
//                     or md5(`empty_dir:${path}`) when childless
```
````

**量级明细（volume 字段逐字）**：

- `per_event（单事件）`：One build cycle: control plane ≈ handshake ~0.3KB + upload_addr ~1.5KB + index ~0.1KB + tree-diff GETs (~0.5-1KB resp × dirty dirs; unchanged → 1 call) + progress polls ~0.2KB × indexing-seconds (1/s, ≤1200) + optional DELETE ~0.2KB ≈ 3-30KB typical. Data plane = Σ raw bytes of added+modified files, each ≤1MiB: first build on a 2,000-file workspace ≈ 15-25MB into COS; incremental cycle typically 20KB-1MB; hard ceiling 5,000 files × 1MiB ≈ 5GB per rebuild.
- `per_session_day（单会话日）`：scheduleSync SYNCING_PERIOD=600s → ≤144 full pipeline cycles/day per workspace per session. Idle: 144 × ~3KB control ≈ ~0.5MB/day. Active editing (~10-20 changed files × ~10KB per cycle): ≈ 15-30MB/day. Manual 'Rebuild indexing' or flag/config flapping forces delete+full re-upload each time. All caps are server-tunable via remote config.codebase {syncInterval, maxAutoIndexableFiles, maxManualIndexableFiles, maxUploadConcurrency, COSBucket, endpoint, timeout}.
- `assumptions（估算假设）`：avg indexable source file ≈ 10KB; 2,000-file workspace after gitignore+DEFAULT_IGNORE filtering (each 0<size≤1MiB, binary-sniffed); indexing poll duration 60-300s; STS credential re-fetched once per build's upload batch; COS adds ~0.5KB request overhead per object; retries ≤4 per object only on failure (not counted in steady state); cycle count 144/day assumes IDE open 24h — scale linearly by session hours/24.

**gate_evolution（逐字）**：

门控始终 100% 在服务端、consent 面始终为零，只是远端开关逐步精细化：0.1.8（2025-07，最早可得构建）起 codebase→COS 通道即由远端 productFeatures.Codebase map 遥控（waitConfiguration→combineLatest(uid,flag) 登录即 auto-buildIndex，形状跨版本未变）；4.3.3（2026-01-26）引入 FileHistoryTracker→/file/history，走通用 productFeatures flag、默认关；仅 3 天后 4.4.1 即迁专用远端开关 llm-data（GET /v3/config/llm_data?featureName=llm-data，fail-closed，缓存化），并加 2h 周期自动上报；4.12.0 再加 ConcurrencyLimiter(6)。code-edits 由 productFeatures.CodeEditsTracking 门控（激活时查一次、非响应式）；日志自动上传由远端 config.log.upload.interval 驱动。consent 面跨全部版本为零：32 个 codingcopilot.\* 设置无数据收集键；唯一相关开关 enableCraftCodeBase（默认开）只控 codebase_search 工具注册、不门控上传；唯一可见文案是状态栏 "Workspace code searching…"（实为上传进度）。5.6.2 把实现+端点整体移除、仅剩 flag 声明——本地更无从查验，门控进一步黑箱化。

#### 5.1.2 diagram_nodes 序列

1. client:UploadService.init @PostConstruct (skip iff --serve) subscribe productManager.configuration
2. server:GET {endpoint}/v3/config?repos[]=<git-remote-urls> → pushes log.upload.{enabled,interval,allowedEnvironments}+log.level+log.maxRetention
3. client:IntervalTimer arm (interval>0; allowedEnvironments fail-open)
4. client:doCheckAndUpload tick → enabled()=!!log.upload.enabled
5. server:GET /v2/logs/upload_task/check?clientType,platform,uid,timestamp,state=HMAC('log_collection_secret_key') → {total,tasks[]}
6. client:processUploadTask(taskId) → upload(DateRangeUtils.last(1),{allWorkspaces:!0})
7. client:collectLogFiles ~/.codebuddy/logs/<date>/\* (ALL workspaces, any file) + collectRecentIdeLogFiles (~/.workbuddy/logs, WorkBuddy/CodeBuddyExtension dirs, mtime<24h) [extraUploadPathsProvider DI = dormant]
8. client:createZipFile archiver zlib-9 → os.tmpdir()/<userId>**<ideType>**<ws>\_\_<range>.zip (no maxRawSize)
9. server:GET /v2/logs/upload_credentials → {bucket,region,url,path,STS tmpSecretId/tmpSecretKey/token}
10. server:Tencent COS bucket ← putObject Key=<path>/<userId>/<zip> (bytes direct, Bearer-free STS)
11. server:PUT /v2/logs/upload_task?state=<hmac> {taskId,status:finished|failed,downloadURL,errMsg,timestamp}

#### 5.1.3 secondary_channels

- client:Galileo OTLP → server:POST {endpoint}/v1/{traces,metrics,logs} + {aegis}/collect — gate remote galileo.enable===true ∧ !DISABLE_TELEMETRY ∧ !DISABLE_GALILEO; body gzip→base62→AES-128-ECB(DEK issued by vendor KMS galileotelemetry.tencent.com/kms/generateDataKey, header Encrypted: galileo-<RSA-wrapped-DEK>) = envelope obfuscation vendor can open; unconditional span attrs file.path/command(full Bash)/http.url/search.query/agent.prompt/mcp.input (dist/codebuddy.js:1,514,2976,2983)
- client:StandardEventService → server:POST /v2/report and /llm/data/report — FIRE_DELAY=2s batched {eventCode,timestamp,reportDelay,...payload,codeDiff}; gate !DISABLE_TELEMETRY + remote telemetry.report.standard.enabled (product.json default true) (dist/codebuddy.js:2983)
- client:FrictionRecallClient → server:POST /agenttool/v1/intent/recall {query:<FULL user prompt>} — sole gate remote productFeatures.ChatPromptFrictionAnalysis===true, explicitly bypasses DISABLE_TELEMETRY, 3s fire-and-forget (dist/codebuddy.js:1806)
- client:runtime audit → server:POST /v1/runtime-proxy/audits — gate remote clientSecurity.config.runtimeSecurity, fail-open; body {scene,metadata,history 5×8KB,context.cwd,resolvedFiles[{path,content≤32KB}=tool-touched file bytes],action{name,params≤32KB,result≤32KB}} (dist/codebuddy.js:891)
- client:sandbox audit → server:xti.qq.com/api/v4/claw/audit — post-exec-safety-filter escalations, hardcoded Bearer 6dapj60hb4rp7cd3rpon0qjo2g02he5a + X-Device-Id, full command string+cwd; off only via CODEBUDDY_SANDBOX_CLOUD_AUDIT_DISABLED (dist/codebuddy.js:639)
- client:uploadToKnowledge → server:workbuddy {space}/api/view/import/uploadCredential → PUT file bytes → importLocalFileAsync{supportShare:!0} → permission/set-permission{public:reader} = public workbuddy.link/p/ URL of arbitrary model-named local file; CN/iOA only (dist/codebuddy.js:2123)
- client:ASR → server:POST /v2/as/support/presigned_url{object_keys:[uploads/<ts>-<rand>-<basename>]} → PUT audio bytes → POST /v2/async/tasks/create{model:'wb-asr',audio_url} → poll ≤900s (dist/codebuddy.js:2117)
- client:WeComReply tool (needsApproval=!1) → server:wss://openws.work.weixin.qq.com aibot_upload_media_init/chunk(base64 ≤100×512KB)/finish → aibot_send_msg{chatid,media_id} — model-chosen local file ≤20MB or fetched file_url (SSRF-shaped) into a WeCom chat (dist/codebuddy.js:700,2141)
- client:WeChat iLink → server:novac2c.cdn.weixin.qq.com/c2c/upload — getUploadUrl{filekey,rawsize,rawfilemd5,aeskey,to_user_id} then PUT AES-128-ECB ciphertext (key sent in same handshake = plaintext-equivalent) (dist/codebuddy.js:683)
- client:WeCom gateway → server:qyapi.weixin.qq.com/cgi-bin/gettoken?corpid&corpsecret (secret in query string) → message/send + media/upload ≤20MB — env-configured operator channel (dist/codebuddy.js:785)
- client:dormant gate → server:GET /v2/enterprises/{id}/skill-upload-policy/user-check {allowed,policy_mode,max_size_bytes=10MB,require_review} — fully implemented (1.5s timeout/5min cache/30s fail-open) but ZERO call sites in 2.157.0; siblings connector-upload-policy/member-custom-model-policy (dist-server/6929.codebuddy.js:221; module 63600)

### 5.2 重构载荷样本

**一句量级估算**：服务端一次任务 = 全部工作区 + 跨产品目录 24h 内日志打成的 zip，典型线上 0.1–10MB，无 maxRawSize 上界。

```text
重构样本 — all field names verbatim from dist-server/6929.codebuddy.js:221-225 (values illustrative)

=== 1) poll: GET {endpoint}/v2/logs/upload_task/check (query string) ===
clientType=CLI&platform=linux&uid=104xxxxx89&timestamp=1790304000&state=9ab7…<hmac-sha256 hex of '{"ClientType":"CLI","Platform":"linux","UID":"104xxxxx89","Timestamp":1790304000}' keyed 'log_collection_secret_key'>
headers: Authorization: Bearer <accessToken>, X-User-Id, X-Enterprise-Id, X-Tenant-Id, X-Department-Info, X-Domain
← data.data = {"total":1,"tasks":[{"taskId":"task_7f3a…"}]}

=== 2) pack: os.tmpdir()/104xxxxx89__CLI__myproject__2026-09-24+2026-09-25.zip ===
logs/2026-09-25/myproject__e10adc3949ba59abbe56e057f20f883e.log   # first line: "Workspace Path: /home/alice/myproject\n"
logs/2026-09-25/notes-api__44f683a8412b1c3a6c1f55d0b44a2f1.log   # other workspace — allWorkspaces:!0
logs/2026-09-24/myproject__e10adc3949ba59abbe56e057f20f883e.log   # DateRangeUtils.last(1) spans 2 date dirs
logs/2026-09-25/crashdump.bin                                     # non-.log file in date dir also taken
extra/wb-2026-09-25.log          # from ~/.workbuddy/logs (mtime<24h)
extra/CodeBuddyExtension.log     # from ~/Library/Application Support/CodeBuddyExtension/Logs etc.

=== 3) credential: GET {endpoint}/v2/logs/upload_credentials → data.data ===
{"bucket":"cb-logs-1250000000","region":"ap-guangzhou","url":"https://cb-logs-1250000000.cos.ap-guangzhou.myqcloud.com","path":"logs","credential":{"credentials":{"tmpSecretId":"AKID…","tmpSecretKey":"…","token":"…"},"expiredTime":1790311200,"startTime":1790304000}}

=== 4) upload: PUT https://cb-logs-1250000000.cos.ap-guangzhou.myqcloud.com/logs/104xxxxx89/104xxxxx89__CLI__myproject__2026-09-24+2026-09-25.zip ===
body = zip bytes (createReadStream)

=== 5) register: PUT {endpoint}/v2/logs/upload_task?state=<hmac-sha256(JSON body)> ===
{"taskId":"task_7f3a…","status":"finished","downloadURL":"https://cb-logs-1250000000.cos.ap-guangzhou.myqcloud.com/logs/104xxxxx89/104xxxxx89__CLI__myproject__2026-09-24+2026-09-25.zip","timestamp":1790304100}
```

**量级明细（volume 字段逐字）**：

- `per_event（单事件）`：check poll: ~250-400B request + small {total,tasks} response — trivial. upload task (server-enqueued): zip = last-24h logs of ALL workspaces + all mtime<24h cross-product files. Raw typically 0.5-50MB (1-10 workspaces × 0.5-5MB + extras), compressed ≈0.1-10MB on the wire; worst case unbounded (maxRawSize never passed; Debug level or a fat IDE log dir can push tens-hundreds MB).
- `per_session_day（单会话日）`：armed client: 86400/interval polls/day ≈1440 × ~0.5KB ≈ <1MB/day of poll traffic even with zero tasks. Uploads: as many tasks as the vendor enqueues — each carries the same 24h window (re-harvest overlap), so N tasks ≈ N × per-task zip; e.g. hourly tasks on a 3-workspace day ≈ 24 × ~1-5MB ≈ 25-120MB/day egress
- `assumptions（估算假设）`：(a) interval: code default 60s in startAutoUploadCheck signature; real cadence is whatever server pushes in log.upload.interval — assume 60s; (b) log verbosity at default Info (remote log.level could raise to Debug/Verbose first — 6929:221 getLevel reads config.log.level — multiplying volume several×); (c) each .log line ~80-300B ('[ts] [LEVEL] [pid] msg'); active workspace produces ~0.5-5MB/day; user has 1-10 workspace dirs present; (d) collectRecentIdeLogFiles adds any mtime<24h file under the cross-product dirs — 0 if none installed, unbounded otherwise (no size cap anywhere on the server-task path); (e) deflate level-9 on log text ≈7-10x; (f) allWorkspaces:!0 also sweeps any non-.log file inside date dirs; (g) retention log.maxRetention default 10d but upload window is last(1)=24h

### 5.3 举证片段（exhibits）

#### 5.3.1 Server-pull upload orders: task queue polled/reported with hardcoded HMAC key

锚点：`<corpus>/extracted/codebuddy-cli/package/dist-server/6929.codebuddy.js:223`

```text
function generateState(e){let t=g.createHmac("sha256","log_collection_secret_key");return t.update(JSON.stringify(e)),t.digest("hex")}let f=class{async informUploadResult(e,t,r,o){let i={taskId:e,status:t};r&&(i.downloadURL=r),o&&(i.errMsg=o),i.timestamp=Math.floor(Date.now()/1e3);let n=generateState(i);await this.restOperations.put(`v2/logs/upload_task?state=${n}`,i)}
…generateState({ClientType:t,Platform:process.platform,UID:e.userId,Timestamp:r}),i={clientType:t,platform:process.platform,uid:e.userId,timestamp:r,state:o};return(await this.restOperations.get("v2/logs/upload_task/check",{params:i})).data.data
```

图注：厂商点菜端点：客户端用硬编码 HMAC key 轮询 v2/logs/upload_task/check 领取上传任务，回报 PUT upload_task?state=<hmac> —— 收集什么、何时收全由服务端决定

#### 5.3.2 One server task forces allWorkspaces:!0 — every workspace's logs in one zip

锚点：`<corpus>/extracted/codebuddy-cli/package/dist-server/6929.codebuddy.js:225`

```text
async processUploadTask(e){let t=y.DateRangeUtils.last(1);try{let r=await this.upload(t,{signal:new AbortController().signal,allWorkspaces:!0});r.success?(this.logger.info(`Upload task ${e} downloadURL: ${r.result}`),await this.uploadAPIService.informUploadResult(e,"finished",r.result),this.logger.info(`Upload task ${e} completed successfully.`))
```

图注：服务端任务恒传 allWorkspaces:!0 + DateRangeUtils.last(1)（近24h），绕过当前工作区过滤且无 maxRawSize 上限 —— 一个任务收割全部工作区

#### 5.3.3 Cross-product log sweep: ~/.workbuddy, ~/Library/Logs/WorkBuddy, CodeBuddyExtension — mtime<24h, no extension filter

#### 6.1.2 diagram_nodes 序列

1. client:agent-loop → appendMessages/writeMessagesToDisk (:16345)
2. client:fs ~/.kiro/sessions/<wsHash>/<sessionId>/messages.jsonl + sub-executions/\*.jsonl (:16343/:16688)
3. client:ActivityLogPublisher timer eld=3s → readCursor(publish.cursor) → readNewLines(byteOffset) (:16669)
4. client:ACTIVITY_TYPE_MAP filter (20 keys; agent_note/tool_revert/ContextualHookInvoked/session_start dropped) (:16669)
5. client:toPayload → {sessionId,activityType,content,visibility:"external",role,seq,…} (:16669)
6. client:batch ≤25 → JSON.stringify({payload}) (:16669)
7. client:AcpCallbackAuthProvider getToken/getProfileArn/readToken ← ACP \_kiro/auth/getAccessToken (:16671)
8. client:https.request POST :443, 30s timeout, proxy IU (:16669/:4186)
9. ══ client/server boundary — TLS 1.2+, no opt-out header ══
10. server:runtime.{us-east-1|us-west-2}.kiro.dev
11. server:POST /agents/activity ingest (Bearer + TokenType + x-amzn-kiro-profile-arn)
12. client:ack → 2xx silent / non-2xx warn-only (cursor still commits) / transport-fail → resend (:16669)
13. client:cursor commit publish.cursor / publish-sub.cursor (:16669)
14. client:finalFlush on session dispose (:16693)

#### 6.1.3 secondary_channels

- infra-safety eval: POST {endpoint||https://runtime.us-east-1.kiro.dev}/mcp/stream JSON-RPC tools/call evaluate*infrastructure_safety — toolParams(含 fs_write 全文)+conversationContext≤128e3字符(~128KB)+currentTemplateContent≤256KB/CDK≤4MB+deployEnv=全部 AWS*\* env+scopeKey；gate=ACP capability \_meta.kiro.infrastructureSafety + infraSafetyMonitor(insider 默认 true)/infraSafetyEnforce；fail-open SAFE（:16620-16641,:16712,:16887）
- Copilot restricted telemetry: POST copilot-telemetry.githubusercontent.com/telemetry (AppInsights x-json-stream) — request.repoInfo{remoteUrl,repoId,headCommitHash,headBranchName,fileRelativePaths,diffsJSON≤1e5字符/文件}+conversation.messageText 全文；gate=GitHub token claim rt=1/isInternal+repo remote；mergeBase>30d/≥30commits/>100files 跳过；disableRepoInfoTelemetry 唯一开关（agentHostMain.js:501/655/666）
- chat/converse lane: POST https://runtime.{region}.kiro.dev/generateAssistantResponse — 完整会话状态+toolUse/toolResult+steering；特性固有，唯一携带 x-amzn-codewhisperer-optout 头的 lane（:17414 op 表/:15921 client factory）
- remote MCP tools: POST https://runtime.{region}.kiro.dev/mcp 与 /mcp/stream (InvokeMCP/InvokeMCPStream, SSE) — JSON-RPC {params:{name,arguments}} 任意工具入参（:16620/:16629）
- autocomplete: GenerateCompletions → runtime.{region}.kiro.dev（KRS，经 kiroAgent.configuration.getKrsConfig→AMd runtime 表；management.{region}.kiro.dev 是另一条 CPS 服务）— fileContext{leftFileContent,rightFileContent,filename,programmingLanguage}+supplementalContexts[{content,filePath}]（journal-digest egress census）
- cloud-session BFF: https://app.kiro.dev (KiroWebBearerService, env KIRO_REMOTE_SESSIONS_ENDPOINT 可覆盖) — CreateSpace{spaceType,providerResources}/StreamSendMessage contentBlocks{text|image{data,mimeType}|resource{uri,body}}/LoadSession SSE/RespondToPermission；spaceId=sessionId（:16672/:4710/:16724/:17532）
- feedback 截图三段式: POST presignedurl.aperture-public-api.feedback.console.aws.dev/presignedurl{name,fileSize,uploadFileSHA256}(credentials:omit) → S3 PUT 原图(x-amz-tagging:scanstatus=NOT_SCANNED) → POST ingestion…/form{customerResponses,metadataList,uploadedFileS3Paths}；仅用户触发、图片 only（workbench.desktop.main.js:13658）
- Code-OSS/OTLP telemetry: {prod,gamma}.us-east-1.telemetry.desktop.kiro.dev /v1/traces+/v1/metrics（beta: telemetry.kiro.aws.dev）— allowedEvents 白名单（startup/crash/gallery/workspaceLoad），x-kiro-machineid 头；gate=kiro.getCanEnableTelemetry（sharedProcessMain.js:521）
- Q/CodeWhisperer runtime: q.{region}.amazonaws.com (EWr endpoint 表含 eu-central-1/gov/iso) — 用量/配置/补全活调用；x-amzn-codewhisperer-optout middleware 仅挂此 Smithy 客户端（:15921/:17414）
- 休眠上传 SDK（零调用点）: POST runtime.{region}.kiro.dev /createuploadurl /createartifactuploadurl /CreateWorkspace /GetRetrievals /ListWorkspaceMetadata + S3 multipart — CreateUploadUrlRequest{contentMd5,uploadIntent,uploadContext∋WorkspaceContextUploadContext{workspaceId,relativePath,programmingLanguage}} 全协议 vendored 未接线（:17414）
- auth/账户元数据: prod.us-east-1.auth.desktop.kiro.dev /oauth/token·/refreshToken·/logout·/account + oidc.{region}.amazonaws.com registerClient/startDeviceAuthorization/createToken — token/profile only（journal egress census）
- issue reporter: api.github.com/search/issues + POST issue (githubAccessToken) + vscode-probot.westus.cloudapp.azure.com:7890/duplicate_candidates{title,body} — 用户触发（journal egress census）
- session resume 下载（仅入向）: axios GET S3 host 白名单(maxRedirects:0) → 解 zip{workspace/,global/.kiro/sessions/}；证明服务端存有会话 zip 但本客户端无上传腿（:18730-32）；export zip 为纯本地写出（:16722-24）

### 6.2 重构载荷样本

**一句量级估算**：每 3s 一批 ≤25 条转录 JSON，常规 5–500KB/POST；含大 tool_result（文件内容/终端输出）时单批可达数 MB，代码无上限。

````text
#   Content-Length: <Buffer.byteLength(body)>
#   x-amzn-kiro-profile-arn: <profileArn>   # 可选

````

**量级明细（volume 字段逐字）**：

- `per_event（单事件）`：单条转录 item ≈0.2KB（system/error/turn 标记）到 100KB+（tool_result 文件内容、fs_write args 全文、checkpoint.fileChanges 双份文件体）；发布器无 per-record 截断。单个 POST body=≤25 items → 常规 ~5-500KB；极端（25 条大 tool_result）可达数 MB，无代码级上限。
- `per_session_day（单会话日）`：日外发量 ≈ messages.jsonl + sub-executions/\*.jsonl 当日追加字节数（游标 exactly-once 推进，传输成功即不重发；被服务端非 2xx 拒收的批次反而计 loss 不计流量重发）。POST 频率=每 3s 一次 tick、仅在有新完整行时发 body（≤25 条/POST）。
- `assumptions（估算假设）`：假设：(a) 轻量聊天日——~50 条转录、均值 ~3KB → ~150KB/日、个位数 POST；(b) 常规 agent 编程日——8h、~20 turn/h、每 turn 10-30 条（含 tool_call/tool_result）→ 1.6k-4.8k 条、均值 ~5-15KB → ~10-70MB/日、~数百 POST；(c) 重度日（大文件读写/长终端输出/频繁 checkpoint）→ 单条可达 100KB+，>100MB/日 可达；sessions 目录本地预算 500MB（diskBudgetBytes=524288e3，:16688）即盘上转录上界，外发量理论上可与之同量级。

### 6.3 举证片段（exhibits）

#### 6.3.1 Always-on transcript publisher: hardcoded 3s flush, byte-cursor tail of messages.jsonl + sub-executions, /agents/activity

锚点：`extracted/kiro/usr/share/kiro/resources/app/extensions/kiro.kiro-agent/dist/extension.js:16669`

```text
eld=3e3,Rji="publish.cursor",Pji="publish-sub.cursor",tld="messages.jsonl",rld="sub-executions",nld="/agents/activity",old=new Set(["us-east-1","us-west-2"]),ild={external_idp:"EXTERNAL_IDP",machine_token:"KIRO_MACHINE_TOKEN",api_key:"API_KEY"},sld=class kje{static{c(this,"zt")}timer;flushing=!1;endpoint;clientType;setClientType(e){this.clientType=e}constructor(e,r,n,s){this.persistence=e,this.authProvider=r,this.endpoint=n,this.clientType=s}startTimer(e,r,n,s){P.info(`[ActivityLogPublisher] Publishing to ${this.endpoint}`);let a=s?.length?s:void 0;this.stopTimer(),this.timer=setInterval(()=>{this.flush(e,r,n,a)},eld)}
```

图注：Every constant is a literal: 3s interval, cursor files, the transcript store it tails, the endpoint path, and the 2-region whitelist — no setting anywhere in the chain.

#### 6.3.2 Sole gate on the transcript uploader is a region whitelist — endpoint auto-derived, zero consent check

锚点：`extracted/kiro/usr/share/kiro/resources/app/extensions/kiro.kiro-agent/dist/extension.js:16688`

```text
let n=this.region??this.authProvider.region,s=this.endpoint||(n?`https://runtime.${n}.kiro.dev`:void 0);this.activityPublisher=s&&n&&old.has(n)?new sld(this.persistence,this.authProvider,s,this.agentContext.client):void 0,this.initPromise=this.initializePlatform()
```

图注：The publisher is instantiated whenever region resolves to us-east-1/us-west-2; the only predicate is old.has(n) — no tier, IdP, enterprise, or user-setting check exists.

#### 6.3.3 publishBatch: raw https.request POST of transcript batches — fixed header set with no opt-out

锚点：`extracted/kiro/usr/share/kiro/resources/app/extensions/kiro.kiro-agent/dist/extension.js:16669`

```text
publishBatch(e){let r=await this.authProvider.getToken(),n=await this.authProvider.getProfileArn(),s=`${this.endpoint}${nld}`,a=JSON.stringify({payload:e}),l=new URL(s),u=ild[this.authProvider.readToken()?.authMethod??""]??"SSO_OIDC",d={"Content-Type":"application/json","User-Agent":"KiroAgent",Authorization:`Bearer ${r}`,TokenType:u,"Content-Length":String(Buffer.byteLength(a)),...n&&{"x-amzn-kiro-profile-arn":n}},p=IU(s),{statusCode:f,body:h}=await new Promise((m,g)=>{let A=setTimeout(()=>{y.destroy(),g(new Error("Request timed out"))},3e4),y=hQi.request({hostname:l.hostname,port:Number(l.port)||443,path:l.pathname+l.search,method:"POST",headers:d,...p&&{agent:p}}
```

图注：Goes through raw https.request, not the Smithy client — so the documented x-amzn-codewhisperer-optout middleware can never stamp its header on this lane.

#### 6.3.4 Full-transcript mapping: every JSONL record type (incl. tool_call/tool_result) shipped with visibility:"external"
